transactions settled in Renminbi, and the reporting currency of the       幣結算，本集團的報告貨幣為人民幣。本集團在
Group is Renminbi. The Group’s subsidiaries in Hong Kong and              中國香港及海外子公司分別以當地貨幣作為功能
overseas use local currencies as their functional currencies, including   性貨幣，涉及日元、港元和美元。本集團有部分
Japanese yen, Hong Kong dollar and US dollar. The Group has a             現金及銀行存款以日元、港元和美元計值。本集
partial amount of cash and bank deposits denominated in Japanese          團繼續就外匯風險管理採取審慎政策。於 2023
yen, Hong Kong dollar and US dollar. The Group continues to adopt a       年，本集團並無就外幣交易、資產及負債制定外
conservative approach in its foreign exchange exposure management.        幣對沖政策。本集團定期檢討其所承受的外匯風
In 2023, the Group did not have a foreign currency hedging policy         險，並於有需要時使用衍生金融工具對沖有關風

policies, and enhance its transparency and accountability.

responsible for promoting the Company’s success by directing and          責促進本公司的成功。董事應客觀地作出符合本

in the best interests of the Company.

business and regularly reviews the contribution required from a           事就履行其對本公司的職責而須作出的貢獻以及

the Director is spending sufficient time performing them that are         責相稱的職責。董事會的執行董事及非執行董事

Responsibilities, Accountabilities and Contributions of                   董事會及管理層各自的職責、責任及貢獻
the Board and Management
The Board should assume responsibility for leadership and control         董事會應承擔領導及監控本公司之責任；以及集
of the Company; and is collectively responsible for directing and         體負責指導及監管本公司之事務。
supervising the Company’s affairs.


The Board directly, and indirectly through its committees, leads and      董事會直接及間接透過其委員會，帶領並提供指
provides direction to management by laying down strategies and            導予管理層，制定策略及監察其實施、監察本集
overseeing their implementation, monitors the Group’s operational and     團之營運及財務表現，以及確保設有良好之企業
financial performance, and ensures that sound corporate governance,       管治、內部監控及風險管理系統。
internal control and risk management systems are in place.


All Directors, including non-executive Directors and independent          全體董事（包括非執行董事及獨立非執行董事）擁
non-executive Directors, have brought a wide spectrum of valuable         有廣泛的寶貴業務經驗、知識及專業精神，有助
business experience, knowledge and professionalism to the Board for       於董事會高效及有效運作。獨立非執行董事負責
its efficient and effective functioning. The independent non-executive    確保本公司之高標準監管報告，並在董事會中提
Directors are responsible for ensuring a high standard of regulatory      供平衡，以就企業行動和營運提出有效之獨立判
reporting of the Company and providing a balance in the Board for         斷。
bringing effective independent judgement on corporate actions and
operations.


All Directors have full and timely access to all the information of the   全體董事均可全面並及時取得本公司所有資料以
Company and may, upon request, seek independent professional              及可應要求在適當情況下尋求獨立專業意見，以
advice in appropriate circumstances, at the Company’s expenses for        向本公司履行其職責，費用由本公司承擔。
discharging their duties to the Company.


The Directors shall disclose to the Company details of other offices      董事須向本公司披露彼等擔任之其他職務之詳

Chairman and Chief Executive Officer                                      董事長及首席執行官
Code Provision C.2.1 of the CG Code states that the roles of              企業管治守則的守則條文 C.2.1 列明董事長與首
chairman and chief executive officer should be separate and should        席執行官的角色應有區分，不應由一人同時兼
not be performed by the same individual. Mr. Lyu Yixiong is both the      任。呂義雄先生兼任本公司董事長及首席執行
chairman of the Board and the chief executive officer of the Company.     官。儘管與企業管治守則的守則條文 C.2.1 有所
Notwithstanding the deviation from Code Provision C.2.1 of the CG         偏離，惟鑒於呂義雄先生對本集團業務擁有豐富
Code, given Mr. Lyu Yixiong’s extensive knowledge and experience          知識及經驗，董事會認為，本公司董事長及首席
of the Group’s business, the Board considers that vesting the roles       執行官的角色由一人同時兼任有利於確保本集團
of both chairman of the Board and chief executive officer of the          內部的領導一致，並為本集團實現更有效及更有
Company in the same person brings the benefit of ensuring consistent      效率的整體策略規劃。董事會相信，現時安排下
leadership within the Group and enabling more effective and efficient     的權力與權限平衡不會受到損害，而此架構將使
overall strategic planning for the Group. The Board believes that the     本公司能夠迅速有效地作出決策並予以執行。
balance of power and authority for the present arrangement will not
be impaired and this structure will enable the Company to make and
implement decisions promptly and efficiently.


The Board will nevertheless continue to review the structure from time    儘管如此，董事會將繼續不時檢討有關架構，並
to time and consider the appropriate move to take when appropriate.       考慮在適當時採取適當舉措。

The Company has adopted a Board Diversity Policy in order to            為提高董事會效率並維持企業管治的高標準，本

standard of corporate governance.

of factors, including but not limited to gender, age, cultural and      景、行業經驗、技術能力、專業資格及技能、知

professional qualifications and skills, knowledge and length of         決定將根據獲選候選人的長處及對董事會的貢獻
service. The ultimate decision will be based on merit of the selected   作出。

The Directors obtained degrees in various majors including business     融、會計、經濟學及法學在內的多個專業的學
administration, finance, accounting, economics and laws. The            位。本公司擁有三名具有不同行業背景的獨立非

SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS                                                        股東權利

Convening an Extraordinary General Meeting                                  召集臨時股東大會
Pursuant to Article 47 of the Articles, Shareholders severally or jointly   根據公司章程第47條，單獨或合計持有本公司股
holding ten percent (10%) or more of shares of the Company have the         份百分之十(10%)或以上的股東有權向董事會請
right to request the Board of Directors to convene an extraordinary         求召開臨時股東大會，並應當以書面形式向董事
general meeting and such request shall be made in writing to the            會提出。董事會應當根據法律、行政法規及公司
Board of Directors. The Board of Directors shall, in accordance with        章程的規定，在收到請求後十(10)日內提出同意
laws, administrative regulations and the Articles of Association, reply     或不同意召開臨時股東大會的書面反饋意見。
in writing as to whether the Board of Directors agrees or disagrees
on the convening of the extraordinary general meeting within ten (10)
days upon the receipt of the requisition.


If the Board of Directors agrees to convene an extraordinary general        董事會同意召開臨時股東大會的，應當在董事會
meeting, a convening notice shall be issued within five (5) days after      決議案後的五(5)日內發出召開大會的通知，通知
the resolution of the Board of Directors. Any modification to the           中對原請求的變更，應當徵得相關股東的同意。
original requisition(s) shall be approved by the relevant shareholders.


If the Board of Directors disagrees to convene the extraordinary            董事會不同意召開臨時股東大會，或者在收到請
general meeting, or fails to issue a reply within ten (10) days upon        求後十(10)日內未作出反饋的，單獨或合計持有
receipt of the requisition(s), shareholders severally or jointly holding    本公司股份百分之十(10%)或以上的股東有權向
ten percent (10%) or more of the shares of the Company are entitled         監事會提議召開臨時股東大會，並應當以書面形
to propose to the Supervisory Committee to convene an extraordinary         式向監事會提出請求。
general meeting, and shall make such requests in writing to the
Supervisory Committee.


If the Supervisory Committee agrees to convene the extraordinary            監事會同意召開臨時股東大會的，應在收到請求
general meeting, a convening notice will be issued within five (5) days     後五(5)日內發出召開大會的通知，通知中對原請
upon the receipt of the request, and any modification to the original       求的變更，應當徵得相關股東的同意。
requisition(s) shall be approved by relevant shareholders.


In the event that the Supervisory Committee fails to issue the              監事會未在規定期限內發出召開通知的，視為監
convening notice within the prescribed period, the Supervisory              事會不召集或主持股東大會，連續九十(90)日以
Committee will be deemed as not to convene or preside the general           上單獨或合計持有本公司股份百分之十(10%)或
meeting. Shareholders severally or jointly holding ten percent (10%) or     以上的股東可以自行召集及主持股東大會。
more of the shares of the Company for consecutive ninety (90) days
may convene the general meeting and preside over on their own.

The RSU Scheme                                                              限制性股票單位激勵計劃

The shareholders of the Company approved the adoption of the                本公司股東於2023年12月14日舉行的2023年第
RSU Scheme at the Second 2023 EGM held on 14 December 2023                  二次臨時股東大會上批准採納本公司的限制性股
(the “Adoption Date”) for the purpose of attracting new talents and         票單位激勵計劃（「採納日期」），旨在吸引新人才
retaining employees whose contributions are important to the long-          及挽留對本集團的長期發展及成功有重要貢獻的
term growth and success of the Group, recognising and rewarding             僱員，表彰及獎勵僱員參與者對本集團的貢獻，
Employee Participants for their contributions to the Group, providing       為僱員參與者提供獲得本公司自有權益的機會，
Employee Participants with the opportunity to acquire proprietary           並鼓勵僱員參與者進一步為本公司作出貢獻，努
interests in the Company and encouraging Employee Participants to           力提升本公司及其股份的價值，造福本公司及其
further contribute to the Company and work towards enhancing the            股東的整體利益。
value of the Company and its Shares for the benefit of the Company
and its Shareholders as a whole.

 於釐定虧損撥備時，個別評估的貿易應收款項預期信貸虧損
 率乃由管理層按客戶的相應外部違約數據、彼等的還款及違
 約歷史以及與彼等的持續業務關係而釐定。就綜合評估的貿
 易應收款項而言，預期信貸虧損率乃按過往的信貸虧損而釐
 定。損失率其後會作出調整，以反映當前及前瞻性資料，如
 影響客戶結付應收款項能力的宏觀經濟因素。

because the adverse consequences of doing so would reasonably
be expected to outweigh the public interest benefits of such
communication.

2. Accounting Policies                                                   2. 會計政策

   2.1 Basis of Preparation                                                 2.1 編製基準
       These financial statements have been prepared in                        該等財務報表已按照國際會計準則
       accordance with International Financial Reporting                       理事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒
       Standards (“IFRSs”) (which include all International                    佈 之 國 際 財 務 報 告 準 則（「 國 際 財 務
       Financial Reporting Standards, International Accounting                 報告準則」）
                                                                                    （包括所有國際財務報告
       Standards (“IASs”) and Interpretations) issued by                       準 則 、 國 際 會 計 準 則（「 國 際 會 計 準
       International Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”) and               則」）及詮釋）及香港公司條例的披露
       the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies                  規定編製。該等財務報表採用歷史成
       Ordinance. They have been prepared under the historical                 本慣例編製，惟已按公平值計量的按
       cost convention, except for financial assets at fair value              公平值計入損益的金融資產除外。該
       through profit or loss which have been measured at                      等財務報表乃以人民幣（「人民幣」）
       fair value. These financial statements are presented in                 呈列，除非另有說明，否則所有金額
       Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to the                      均四捨五入至最接近的千位整數。
       nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.


       Basis of consolidation                                                  綜合基準
       The consolidated financial statements include the                       綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公
       financial statements of the Company and its subsidiaries                司（統稱「本集團」）截至2023年12月
       (collectively referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended            31 日止年度的財務報表。附屬公司
       31 December 2023. A subsidiary is an entity (including a                為本公司直接或間接控制的實體（包
       structured entity), directly or indirectly, controlled by the           括結構性實體）。當本集團對參與投
       Company. Control is achieved when the Group is exposed,                 資對象業務的浮動回報承擔風險或享
       or has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with            有權利以及能透過對投資對象的權力
       the investee and has the ability to affect those returns               （即本集團獲賦予現有能力以主導投
       through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights              資對象相關活動的既存權利）影響該
       that give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant          等回報時，即取得控制權。
       activities of the investee).


       Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting             一般情況下，有一個推定，即多數投
       rights results in control. When the Company has, less than              票權形成控制權。倘本公司直接或間
       a majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee,              接擁有投資對象少於過半數的投票權
       the Group considers all relevant facts and circumstances                或類似權利，則本集團於評估其是否
       in assessing whether it has power over an investee,                     擁有對投資對象的權力時會考慮一切
       including:                                                              相關事實及情況，包括：


       (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote                   (a)    與投資對象其他投票持有人的
             holders of the investee;                                                 合約安排；


       (b)   rights arising from other contractual arrangements;               (b)    其他合約安排所產生的權利；
             and                                                                      及


       (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.            (c)    本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

            The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared          附屬公司的財務報表乃採用貫徹一致

            consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries        間而編製。附屬公司的業績由本集團

            obtains control, and continue to be consolidated until the         綜合入賬至該控制權終止當日。
            date that such control ceases.


            Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive           損益及其他全面收入的各組成部分歸
            income are attributed to the owners of the parent of the           屬於本集團母公司擁有人及非控股權
            Group and to the non-controlling interests, even if this           益，即使此舉引致非控股權益出現虧
            results in the non-controlling interests having a deficit          絀結餘。所有集團內公司間資產及負
            balance. All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity,           債、權益、收入、開支以及與本集團
            income, expenses and cash flows relating to transactions           成員公司之間交易有關的現金流量均
            between members of the Group are eliminated in full on             於綜合入賬時悉數抵銷。
            consolidation.


            The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an                 倘事實及情況反映上述三項控制權因
            investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there are        素中一項或多項有變，則本集團會重
            changes to one or more of the three elements of control            估是否仍然控制投資對象。附屬公司
            described above. A change in the ownership interest of a           擁有權權益的變動（並無失去控制權）
            subsidiary, without a loss of control, is accounted for as         入賬為權益交易。
            an equity transaction.


            If the Group loses control over a subsidiary, it                   倘本集團失去對一間附屬公司的控制
            derecognises the related assets (including goodwill),              權，則其終止確認相關資產（包括商
            liabilities, any non-controlling interest and the exchange         譽）、負債、任何非控股權益及累計
            fluctuation reserve cumulative; and recognises the                 匯兌變動儲備；及於損益中確認任何
            fair value of any investment retained and any resulting            保留投資的公平值及任何因而產生的
            surplus or deficit in profit or loss. The Group’s share of         盈餘或虧絀。先前於其他全面收入內
            components previously recognised in other comprehensive            確認的本集團應佔部分按假設本集團
            income is reclassified to profit or loss or retained profits,      直接出售相關資產或負債所須採用的
            as appropriate, on the same basis as would be required if          相同基準，視乎情況重新分類至損益
            the Group had directly disposed of the related assets or           或保留盈利。
            liabilities.

        The nature and the impact of the new and revised IFRSs                    該等適用於本集團的新訂及經修訂國

        a)      Amendments to IAS 1 require entities to disclose                  a)     國際會計準則第 1 號（修訂本）

                than their significant accounting policies. Accounting                   資料，而非重大會計政策。倘
                policy information is material if, when considered                       連同實體財務報表內其他資料

                financial statements, it can reasonably be expected                      合理預期會影響通用財務報表

   2.4 Material Accounting Policies                                       2.4 主要會計政策
       Investments in associates and joint ventures                          於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
       An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long-              聯營公司指本集團長期持有一般不少
       term interest of generally not less than 20% of the equity            於20%股本投票權並對其有重大影響
       voting rights and over which it has significant influence.            力的實體。重大影響力指參與投資對
       Significant influence is the power to participate in the              象的財務及經營政策的權力，而並非
       financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but          控制或共同控制該等政策。
       is not control or joint control over those policies.


       A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby                合營企業為一項合營安排，據此，擁
       the parties that have joint control of the arrangement                有安排共同控制權的人士均有權享有
       have rights to the net assets of the joint venture. Joint             合營企業的資產淨值。共同控制為合
       control is the contractually agreed sharing of control of an          約協定應佔安排控制權，其僅在相關
       arrangement, which exists only when decisions about the               活動決策必須獲應佔控制權的人士一
       relevant activities require the unanimous consent of the              致同意方存在。
       parties sharing control.


       The Group’s investments in associates and joint ventures              本集團於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
       are stated in the consolidated statement of financial                 以本集團應佔資產淨值減任何減值虧
       position at the Group’s share of net assets under the                 損，按權益會計法於綜合財務狀況表
       equity method of accounting, less any impairment losses.              列賬。

       Business combinations and goodwill                                    業務合併及商譽

       the acquiree. For each business combination, the Group                否以公平值或被收購方可識別資產淨
       elects whether to measure the non-controlling interests in            值的應佔比例，計量於被收購方的非
       the acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate share of           控股權益。非控股權益的一切其他成
       the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other components          份乃按公平值計量。收購相關成本於
       of non-controlling interests are measured at fair value.              產生時列為開支。
       Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.


       The Group determines that it has acquired a business                  倘所獲得的一組活動及資產包括一項
       when the acquired set of activities and assets include an             投入及一個實質性過程，兩者共同顯
       input and a substantive process that together significantly           著促進創造產出的能力，則本集團釐
       contribute to the ability to create outputs.                          定其已收購一項業務。

       classification and designation in accordance with the                 關條件，評估將承接的金融資產及負
       contractual terms, economic circumstances and pertinent               債，以作出適合的分類及標示，其中
       conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the              包括將被收購方主合約中的嵌入式衍
       separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of               生工具進行分離。
       the acquiree.

       acquisition date fair value and any resulting gain or loss is         量，而產生的任何收益或虧損於損益
       recognised in profit or loss.                                         內確認。

       Fair value measurement                                               公平值計量
       The Group measures its debt investments at fair value                本集團於各報告期間末透過其他全面
       through other comprehensive income at the end of each                收入按公平值計量其債務投資。公平
       reporting period. Fair value is the price that would be              值乃市場參與者之間於計量日進行的
       received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in         有序交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負
       an orderly transaction between market participants at the            債所支付的價格。公平值乃假設出售
       measurement date. The fair value measurement is based                資產或轉讓負債的交易於該資產或負
       on the presumption that the transaction to sell the asset            債的主要市場（倘無主要市場，則為
       or transfer the liability takes place either in the principal        對該資產或負債最有利的市場）進行
       market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a            而計量。本集團必須可於該主要市場
       principal market, in the most advantageous market for the            或最有利市場進行交易。資產或負債
       asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous           的公平值乃採用市場參與者為資產或
       market must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of            負債定價時所用的假設計量，並假設
       an asset or a liability is measured using the assumptions            市場參與者按本身最佳經濟利益行
       that market participants would use when pricing the asset            事。
       or liability, assuming that market participants act in their
       economic best interest.


       A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes              非金融資產的公平值計量，須衡量市
       into account a market participant’s ability to generate              場參與者最大程度及最佳使用該資產
       economic benefits by using the asset in its highest and              得到經濟利益的能力，或將該資產售
       best use or by selling it to another market participant that         予另一可最大程度及最佳使用該資產
       would use the asset in its highest and best use.                     的市場參與者而獲得經濟利益的能
                                                                            力。


       The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate             本集團採用在有關情況下屬適當的估
       in the circumstances and for which sufficient data are               值技術，且有充足數據可計量公平
       available to measure fair value, maximising the use of               值、盡量使用相關可觀察輸入數據同
       relevant observable inputs and minimising the use of                 時盡量避免使用不可觀察輸入數據。
       unobservable inputs.

            Group as a lessor                                                  本集團作為出租人
            When the Group acts as a lessor, it classifies at lease            當本集團作為出租人時，其於租賃開

            all the risks and rewards incidental to ownership of an            的絕大部分風險及回報的租賃，均分
            asset are classified as operating leases. When a contract          類為經營租賃。當合約包含租賃及非
            contains lease and non-lease components, the Group                 租賃組成部分時，本集團按相對獨立

            Rental income is accounted for on a straight-line basis            入於租期內按直線法入賬，並計入損
            over the lease terms and is included in revenue in the             益表的收入。磋商及安排經營租賃產
            statement of profit or loss due to its operating nature.           生的初步直接成本乃加於租賃資產的
            Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating and arranging         賬面值上，並於租期內按租賃收入相
            an operating lease are added to the carrying amount of             同基準確認。或然租金於賺取期間確

       through profit or loss.

       managing them. With the exception of trade receivables                 無重大融資部分或本集團並未就此應
       that do not contain a significant financing component or               用不調整重大融資部分影響實際權宜
       for which the Group has applied the practical expedient                之計的貿易應收款項外，本集團初步
       of not adjusting the effect of a significant financing                 按其公平值加倘並非按公平值計入
       component, the Group initially measures a financial asset              損益的金融資產，交易成本計量金融
       at its fair value, plus in the case of a financial asset not at        資產。根據下文「收入確認」所載政
       fair value through profit or loss, transaction costs. Trade            策，並無重大融資部分或本集團並未
       receivables that do not contain a significant financing                就此應用實際權宜之計的貿易應收款
       component or for which the Group has applied the                       項乃按國際財務報告準則第15號釐定
       practical expedient are measured at the transaction price              的交易價格計量。
       determined under IFRS 15 in accordance with the policies
       set out for “Revenue recognition” below.


       In order for a financial asset to be classified and                    為使金融資產按攤銷成本或按公平值
       measured at amortised cost or fair value through other                 計入其他全面收入分類及計量，需產

       business model.                                                        論業務模式。

2. Accounting Policies (Continued)                                          2. 會計政策（續）

      2.4 Material Accounting Policies (Continued)                           2.4 主要會計政策（續）
            Impairment of financial assets                                     金融資產減值
            The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit              本集團就並非按公平值計入損益持有
            losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair          的所有債務工具確認預期信貸虧損撥
            value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the                備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根據合約應
            difference between the contractual cash flows due in               付的合約現金流量與本集團預期收取
            accordance with the contract and all the cash flows                的所有現金流量之間的差額，按原有
            that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an                實際利率相近的差額貼現。預期現金
            approximation of the original effective interest rate. The         流量將包括同為合約條款組成部分的
            expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale          自銷售所持有抵押品所得的現金流量
            of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are           或其他提升信貸物品。
            integral to the contractual terms.


            General approach                                                   一般方式
            ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures            預期信貸虧損於兩個階段獲確認。就
            for which there has not been a significant increase in credit      自初步確認起信貸風險並無顯著增加
            risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit       的信貸風險而言，預期信貸虧損提供
            losses that result from default events that are possible           予因未來十二個月內可能發生的違約
            within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those              事件而導致的信貸虧損（十二個月預
            credit exposures for which there has been a significant            期信貸虧損）。就自初步確認起經已
            increase in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss          顯著增加的信貸風險而言，不論何時
            allowance is required for credit losses expected over the          發生違約事件，於餘下風險年期內的
            remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of      信貸虧損均須計提虧損撥備（全期預
            the default (a lifetime ECL).                                      期信貸虧損）。


            At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                 於各報告日期，本集團評估自初步確
            the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased            認後金融工具的信貸風險是否有大幅
            significantly since initial recognition. When making the           增加。當作出評估時，本集團比較於
            assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default               報告日期金融工具發生違約風險與於
            occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting          初步確認日期金融工具發生違約風
            date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial         險，並考慮於沒有過度成本或努力的
            instrument as at the date of initial recognition and               情況下可得的合理及可支持數據，包
            considers reasonable and supportable information that is           括過往及前瞻性資料。倘合約付款逾
            available without undue cost or effort, including historical       期超過30日以上，則本集團認為信貸
            and forward-looking information. The Group considers that          風險大幅增加。
            there has been a significant increase in credit risk when
            contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.

       outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking into           合約款項，則本集團亦可認為金融資
       account any credit enhancements held by the Group.                   產將會違約。

       expectation of recovering the contractual cash flows.                金流量時撇銷。

       comprehensive income and financial assets at amortised               資及按攤銷成本列賬的金融資產，均

       and they are classified within the following stages for              預期信貸虧損的各階段分類，惟貿易

       apply the simplified approach as detailed below.                     的簡化方式。

                        risk has not increased significantly since                   來沒有顯著增加的金融工

                        equal to 12-month ECLs                                       金額計算


       Stage 2    –     Financial instruments for which credit              第二階段 － 自初次確認以來信貸風險
                        risk has not increased significantly since                   並無顯著增加但並非發生
                        initial recognition but that are not credit-                 信貸減值的金融資產的金
                        impaired financial assets and for which                      融工具，其虧損撥備按全

            Simplified approach                                                   簡化方式
            For trade receivables that do not contain a significant               不包含重大融資成份的貿易應收款
            financing component or when the Group applies the                     項，或當本集團對該筆貿易應收款項
            practical expedient of not adjusting the effect of a                  已採用未經調整重大融資成份影響的
            significant financing component, the Group applies                    權宜之計時，本集團採納簡化方式計

            simplified approach, the Group does not track changes in              集團不會追蹤信貸風險的變動，而是
            credit risk, but instead recognises a loss allowance based            根據每個報告日期的全期預期信貸虧

            established a provision matrix that is based on its historical        往信貸虧損經驗建立撥備矩陣，並根
            credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors          據債權人及經濟環境的前瞻性因素作
            specific to the debtors and the economic environment.                 出調整。

       Derecognition of financial liabilities                                 終止確認金融負債
       A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation              當金融負債的責任已履行、取消或屆
       under the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.            滿時，金融負債會被終止確認。

       or the terms of an existing liability are substantially                債所取代，或當現時負債的條款被重
       modified, such an exchange or modification is treated as               大修訂，該取代或修訂被視為對原有
       a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition of         負債的終止確認及對新負債的確認，
       a new liability, and the difference between the respective             而各自賬面值的差額於損益確認。
       carrying amounts is recognised in profit or loss.

       and settle the liabilities simultaneously.

            Provisions                                                        撥備

            to be required to settle the obligation. The increase in the      時間流逝而產生的升幅，乃於損益中

            the present value of the amount receivable, discounted            始起本集團與客戶間的個別融資交易
            using the discount rate that would be reflected in a              中反映的貼現率貼現。當合約包括為

            the customer at contract inception. When the contract             融資組成部分時，根據合約確認的收
            contains a financing component which provides the Group           入包括根據實際利率法在合約負債上
            with a significant financial benefit for more than one year,      累積的利息開支。對於客戶付款及轉
            revenue recognised under the contract includes the                讓承諾的貨品或服務之間的期限為一

            the effective interest method. For a contract where the           融資組成部分的影響而調整，採用國
            period between the payment by the customer and the                際財務報告準則第15號中的實用權宜
            transfer of the promised goods or services is one year or         之計。
            less, the transaction price is not adjusted for the effects

  Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                       租賃 － 估算增量借款利率
  The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit            本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利
  in a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing              率 ， 因 此 ， 使 用 增 量 借 款 利 率（「 增 量 借
  rate (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of        款利率」）計量租賃負債。增量借款利率為
  interest that the Group would have to pay to borrow over a               本集團於類似經濟環境中為取得與使用權
  similar term, and with a similar security, the funds necessary           資產價值相近之資產，而以類似抵押品與
  to obtain an asset of a similar value to the right-of-use asset          類似期間借入所需資金應支付之利率。因
  in a similar economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects            此，增量借款利率反映了本集團「應支付」
  what the Group “would have to pay”, which requires estimation            的利率，當無可觀察的利率時（如就並無訂
  when no observable rates are available (such as for subsidiaries         立融資交易之附屬公司而言）或當其需要作
  that do not enter into financing transactions) or when it needs          出調整以反映租賃的條款及條件時（如租賃
  to be adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease          並非以附屬公司功能貨幣計值），則須作出
  (for example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s functional         利率估計。當可觀察輸入數據可用時，本
  currency). The Group estimates the IBR using observable inputs           集團使用可觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）估
  (such as market interest rates) when available and is required to        算增量借款利率並須作出若干實體特定的
  make certain entity-specific estimates (such as the subsidiary’s         估計（如附屬公司的獨立信貸評級）。
  stand-alone credit rating).

      contain discrete operating segment financial information and the       團的財務業績作為一個整體進行審閱。因
      directors reviewed the financial results of the Group as a whole.      此，並無呈列有關經營分部的進一步資料。
      Therefore, no further information about the operating segment is
      presented.

   Under the Law of the PRC on Enterprise Income Tax (the “EIT            根據《中華人民共和國企業所得稅法》
                                                                                          （「企
   Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the EIT Law, the EIT            業 所 得 稅 法 」）及《 企 業 所 得 稅 法 實 施 條
   rate of the PRC subsidiaries is 25% unless those are subject to        例 》， 中 國 附 屬 公 司 的 企 業 所 得 稅 稅 率 為
   tax exemption set out below.                                           25%，惟獲得下文所載稅項豁免者除外。


   Shanghai Zhongyi Daily Chemical Co., Ltd. was accredited as            上海中翊日化有限公司於2021年至2023年
   an “Advanced Technology Enterprise” from 2021 to 2023, and             被認定為「高新技術企業」，因此本年度上
   therefore Shanghai Zhongyi Daily Chemical Co., Ltd. was entitled       海中翊日化有限公司可按優惠企業所得稅
   to a preferential EIT rate of 15% for the year. This qualification     稅率 15% 納稅。該資質每三年須經中國相
   is subject to review by the relevant tax authority in the PRC for      關稅務部門審核。
   every three years.


   Certain of the Group’s PRC subsidiaries are qualified as small         本集團若干中國附屬公司符合小微企業的
   and micro enterprises and are entitled to a preferential corporate     資格，於年內按優惠企業所得稅稅率 20%
   income tax rate of 20% during the year.                                納稅。

      valuer. Under the valuation models, an income-based approach         式，工業物業採用收入法進行估值。
      has been adopted for industrial properties.


      The income approach is based on the rental income of the             收入法乃基於現有租賃所得及 ╱ 或於現有
      properties derived from the existing leases and/or achievable        市場上可實現的物業租金收入，並已就該
      in the existing market with due allowance for the reversionary       等租賃的復歸收入潛力作出適當撥備，而
      income potential of the leases, which have been then capitalized     有關租賃其後已按適當的資本化比率撥充
      to determine the market value at an appropriate capitalization       資本以釐定市值。
      rate.


      The fair value estimations for the owner-occupied properties         業主自用物業的公平值估計為公平值層級
      were at Level 3 of the fair value hierarchy.                         第三級。


      The fair value measurement is based on the above properties’         公平值計量乃基於上述物業的最高及最佳
      highest and best use, which does not differ from their actual        用途作出，有關用途與彼等的實際用途並
      use.                                                                 無不同。


      During the year ended 31 December 2023, there were no                於截至2023年12月31日止年度，概無轉入
      transfers into or out of Level 3 or any other level.                 或轉出第三級或任何其他層級。

31. Reserves                                                               31. 儲備

   The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements                     本集團於本年度及過往年度的儲備金額及
   therein for the current and prior years are presented in the              其變動於綜合權益變動表中呈列。
   consolidated statement of changes in equity.


   Share-based payment reserve                                               以股份為基礎的付款儲備
   The share-based payment reserve represents reserve arisen                 以股份為基礎的付款儲備指股份激勵計劃
   from share incentive schemes. Details of the movements in the             產生的儲備。以股份為基礎的付款儲備的
   share-based payment reserve are set out in the consolidated               變動詳情載於綜合權益變動表。
   statement of changes in equity.


   Share premium                                                             股份溢價
   The increase in the Group’s share premium for the year was a              於年內，本集團的股份溢價增加乃由於財
   result of listing the Company’s shares on the Hong Kong Stock             務報表附註29 所述本公司股份於香港聯交
   Exchange as described in note 29 to the financial statement.              所上市所致。


   Statutory reserve                                                         法定儲備
   In accordance with the Company Law of the People’s Republic               根據《中華人民共和國公司法》，中國境內
   of China, each company in the PRC is required to allocate 10%             各公司須提取法定稅後利潤的 10% 列入法
   of the statutory after tax profits to the statutory reserve until the     定公積金，直至公積金累計總額達到公司
   cumulative total of the reserve reaches 50% of the company’s              註冊資本的 50%。經中國有關政府部門批
   registered capital. Subject to approval from the relevant PRC             准後，法定儲備可用於抵銷任何累計虧損
   authorities, the statutory reserve may be used to offset any              或增加公司的註冊資本。法定儲備不可用
   accumulated losses or increase the registered capital of the              於向中國附屬公司股東分派股息。
   company. The statutory reserve is not available for dividend
   distribution to shareholders of the PRC subsidiaries.


   Capital reserve                                                           資本儲備
   The capital reserve of the Group represents the contribution              本集團的資本儲備指最終控股公司及股東
   from the ultimate holding company and shareholders, business              的出資、受共同控制的業務合併以及代價
   combinations under the common control and the excess of the               超逾所收購非控股權益賬面值的差額。資
   consideration over the carrying amount of the non-controlling             本儲備變動的詳情載於綜合權益變動表。
   interests acquired. Details of the movements in capital reserve
   are set out in the consolidated statement of changes in equity.


   Exchange fluctuation reserve                                              匯兌波動儲備
   The exchange fluctuation reserve represents exchange                      匯兌波動儲備指換算功能貨幣與本集團呈
   differences arising from the translation of the financial statements      列貨幣不同的海外業務的財務報表所產生
   of foreign operations whose functional currencies are different           的匯兌差額。
   from the Group’s presentation currency.

38. F i n a n c i a l R i s k M a n a g e m e n t                          38. 金融風險管理目標及政策
    Objectives and Policies

    The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included     金融資產及負債的公平值按自願雙方於當
    at the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in              前交易（強制或清算出售除外）中交換有關
    a current transaction between willing parties, other than in a           工具的金額入賬。
    forced or liquidation sale.


    The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise cash and            本集團的主要金融工具包括現金及現金等
    cash equivalents, lease liabilities, and interest-bearing bank           價物、租賃負債及計息銀行及其他借款。
    and other borrowings. The main purpose of these financial                該等金融工具的主要目的是為支持本集團
    instruments is to support the Group’s operations. The Group              的營運。本集團有多項其他金融資產及負
    has various other financial assets and liabilities such as trade         債，如其營運中直接產生的貿易應收款項
    receivables and trade payables which arise directly from its             及貿易應付款項。
    operations.


    The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are        本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為外幣風
    foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The board of      險、信貸風險及流動資金風險。董事會審
    directors reviews and agrees policies for managing these risks           閱及批准管理該等風險的政策，詳情概述
    and they are summarised below.                                           如下。


    Foreign currency risk                                                    外幣風險
    The Group’s major businesses are in Chinese Mainland and                 本集團主要業務位於中國內地及大部分交
    the majority of the transactions are conducted in RMB. Most of           易以人民幣進行。本集團大部分資產及負
    the Group’s assets and liabilities are denominated in RMB. The           債以人民幣計值。於年內，本集團並無重
    Group does not have material foreign currency risk during the            大外幣風險。
    year.


    Credit risk                                                              信貸風險
    The Group trades only with recognised and creditworthy                   本集團僅與獲認可且信譽良好的第三方進
    third parties. It is the Group’s policy that all customers who           行交易。本集團秉持的政策是，所有希望
    wish to trade on credit terms are subject to credit verification         以信貸方式進行交易的客戶須接受信貸審
    procedures. In addition, receivable balances are monitored on            核程序。此外，持續對應收款項結餘進行
    an ongoing basis and the Group’s exposure to bad debts is not            監控，而本集團承受的壞賬風險並不重大。
    significant.

    Capital management                                                        資金管理
    The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are              本集團資金管理的主要目的乃保障本集團
    to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern           有能力持續經營及維持穩健資本比率以支
    and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its            持其業務及使股東價值最大化。
    business and maximise shareholders’ value.


    The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments             本集團管理其資本結構並根據經濟狀況的
    to it in light of changes in economic conditions and the risk             變化及相關資產的風險特徵對其作出調
    characteristics of the underlying assets. To maintain or adjust           整。為保持或調整資本結構，本集團可調
    the capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend                  整向股東派付的股息，向股東歸還資本或
    payment to shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue          發行新股。本集團不受任何外界施加的
    new shares. The Group is not subject to any externally imposed            資金規定所限。截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日及
    capital requirements. No change was made in the objectives,               2022年12月31日止年度，資本管理目標、
    policies or processes for managing capital during the years               政策或流程概無任何變動。
    ended 31 December 2023 and 31 December 2022.


    The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is total          本集團採用資產負債比率（即總債務除以總
    debt divided by the total assets. Total debt includes current             資產）監察資本。總債務包括流動負債及非
    liabilities and non-current liabilities. Total assets include current     流動負債。總資產包括流動資產及非流動
    assets and non-current assets.                                            資產。
